1. Issue type similarity (e.g., all security subprocess issues together)

4. Dependency order (what needs to be fixed first to unlock other fixes)

Count how many issues are in each cluster.

Also identify:
- The highest-severity issues (all "high" priority ones)
- Any import cycles (these are architectural blockers)
- Files with the most issues (hotspots)
- Quick wins (many low-severity issues that can be batch-fixed)

* 70)\nsorted_files = sorted(files_data, key=lambda x: x['actual_count'], reverse=True)\nfor file_data in sorted_files[:30]:\n    print(f\"{file_data['actual_count']:2d} issues: {file_data['file']}\")\nprint()\n\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"ARCHITECTURAL CLUSTERING\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\narch_clusters = defaultdict(list)\nfor file_data in files_data:\n    path = file_data['file']\n    if 'base/' in path:\n        arch_clusters['base/'].append(file_data)\n    elif 'app/commands/' in path:\n        arch_clusters['app/commands/'].append(file_data)\n    elif 'app/output/' in path:\n        arch_clusters['app/output/'].append(file_data)\n    elif 'app/cli_support/' in path:\n        arch_clusters['app/cli_support/'].append(file_data)\n    elif 'app/' in x[1]), reverse=True):\n    total_issues = sum(f['actual_count'] for f in files)\n    print(f\"{arch:30s}: {total_issues:3d} issues across {len(files):3d} files\")\n\nprint()\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"QUICK WIN ANALYSIS (low-severity issues by type)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nquick_wins = defaultdict(int)\nfor file_data in files_data:\n    for issue in file_data['issues']:\n        if issue['severity'] == 'low':\n            if 'subprocess' in issue['description'] or 'B404' in issue['description']:\n                quick_wins['security_subprocess_batch'] += 1\n            elif QUICK WIN 1: Weak MD5 Hash Fixes (7 files, 7 issues) — 10 minutes
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Pattern: All 7 extractors: hashlib.md5() → hashlib.md5(usedforsecurity=False)
Files: treesitter/_extractors.py, typescript/extractors.py, csharp/extractors.py,
       dart/extractors.py, gdscript/extractors.py, go/extractors.py, python/extractors.py
Severity: HIGH (security issue, but simple fix)
Impact: Eliminates 7 HIGH-severity items in one PR


QUICK WIN 2: Subprocess Security Fixes (31 files, 31 issues) — 30-45 minutes
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Pattern: B603/B607/B404 issues in subprocess usage
Files: git_context.py (11 issues), scorecard_parts/meta.py (5), detectors (15)
Severity: LOW (but high volume)
Fix strategy: Replace subprocess.run with Popen + proper arg handling, or use check_call
Impact: Eliminates 31 LOW-severity issues, improves security posture


QUICK WIN 3: Swallowed Errors (13 issues) — 30 minutes
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Pattern: Catch block with only logging (no re-raise)
Files: Scattered across meta.py, apply_flow.py, override_handlers.py, etc.
Fix: Add proper error handling or at least log.error() + re-raise
Impact: ~13 MEDIUM-severity items


Total potential: ~51 issues fixed in isolated PRs without architectural changes


EXECUTION ROADMAP (7 Phases, ~12-16 weeks)
================================================================================

PHASE 1: UNBLOCK CRITICAL PATHS (1-2 weeks) — SERIAL, THEN PARALLEL
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Priority: CRITICAL (blocks all downstream work)
Goal: Resolve 3 import cycles

  STEP 1a. plan_reconcile.py ↔ workflow.py
    • Extract shared state to desloppify/app/commands/scan/_state_model.py
    • Import in both modules; breaks cycle
    • Update tests

  STEP 1b. schema.py ↔ schema_scores.py
    • Define protocol: class ScoringProvider(Protocol): def score(...)
    • schema.py imports protocol (no impl)
    • schema_scores.py implements protocol
    • Break cycle via lazy import in schema.py __init__

  STEP 1c. typescript/__init__.py ↔ _fixers.py
    • Move fixer registry to _fixer_registry.py
    • Both modules import from _fixer_registry
    • Break cycle; enable plugin decomposition

Deliverables: All 3 import cycles resolved; no HIGH blockers from cycles


PHASE 2: SAFETY & DATA INTEGRITY (1-2 weeks) — PARALLEL with Phase 3
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
Priority: HIGH (prevent data loss)
Goal: Fix 7 HIGH-severity dict/schema issues + security batch work

  CLUSTER 2A: Dict Key Issues (base/search/query.py, external.py)
    • Audit: why are "kind", "config", "query_truncated", "session" missing?
    • Add missing keys during construction or document why they're optional
    • Add TypedDict validation to catch at type-check time

  CLUSTER 2B: Schema Drift (synthetic.py, _stage_flow_commands.py)
    • Consolidate dict literal construction into factory functions
    • Use dataclass or TypedDict for schema definition
    • Add tests for schema consistency

  CLUSTER 3A: Weak MD5 Hashes (QUICK WIN #1 — 10 min)
    • Single: hashlib.md5(usedforsecurity=False)
    • 7 extractors in one PR

  CLUSTER 3B: Subprocess Calls (QUICK WIN #2 — 30-45 min)
    • Batch refactor: subprocess module usage across 31 files
    • Use Popen with explicit args or check_call
    • Add shell=False enforcement

  CLUSTER 3C: XML Parsing Security (1 file: csharp/detectors/deps_support.py)
    • Replace xml.etree.ElementTree with defusedxml
    • Or call defusedxml.defuse_stdlib()

   204→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   205→    if file:
   206→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   207→    summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   208→    if summary:
   209→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   210→
   211→
   212→def _load_issues_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   213→    """Load issues from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   214→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   215→    return rt.state.get("issues", {})
   216→
   217→
   218→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   219→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   220→    plan = load_plan()
   221→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   222→    if cluster is None:
   223→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    # Header
   227→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   228→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   229→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   230→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   231→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   232→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   233→    if desc:
   234→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   235→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   236→    if action:
   237→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   238→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   239→    if steps:
   240→        print(colorize(f"  Steps ({len(steps)}):", "dim"))
   241→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   242→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {step}", "dim"))
   243→
   244→    # Members
   245→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   246→    if not issue_ids:
   247→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   248→    else:
   249→        issues = _load_issues_best_effort(args)
   250→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(issue_ids)}):", "dim"))
   251→        for idx, fid in enumerate(issue_ids, 1):
   252→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, issues.get(fid))
   253→
   254→    # Commands
   255→    print()
   256→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   257→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   258→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   259→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   260→
   261→
   262→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   263→    plan = load_plan()
   264→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   265→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   266→    if not clusters:
   267→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   268→        return
   269→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   270→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   271→        member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   272→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   273→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   274→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   275→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   276→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   277→
   278→
   279→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   280→    """Update cluster description and/or action_steps."""
   281→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   282→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
   283→    steps: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "steps", None)
   284→
   285→    if description is None and steps is None:
   286→        print(colorize("  Nothing to update. Use --description and/or --steps.", "yellow"))
   287→        return
   288→
   289→    plan = load_plan()
   290→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   291→    if cluster is None:
   292→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    if description is not None:
   296→        cluster["description"] = description
   297→    if steps is not None:
   298→        cluster["action_steps"] = list(steps)
   299→        print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
   300→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   301→            clean = _LEADING_NUM_RE.sub('', step)
   302→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {clean}", "dim"))
   303→        if len(steps) == 0:
   304→            print(colorize("  Warning: 0 steps stored. Did you forget the --steps values?", "yellow"))
   305→        elif len(steps) == 1 and len(steps[0]) > 100:
   306→            print(colorize("  Warning: only 1 step stored and it's quite long. Check shell quoting.", "yellow"))
   307→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   308→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   309→    append_log_entry(
   310→        plan, "cluster_update", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   311→        detail={"description": description, "steps": steps},
   312→    )
   313→    save_plan(plan)
   314→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   315→
   316→
   317→def _cmd_cluster_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   318→    """Merge source cluster into target cluster."""
   319→    source: str = getattr(args, "source", "")
   320→    target: str = getattr(args, "target", "")
   321→
   322→    plan = load_plan()
   323→    try:
   324→        added, source_ids = merge_clusters(plan, source, target)
   325→    except ValueError as ex:
   326→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   327→        return
   328→
   329→    append_log_entry(
   330→        plan, "cluster_merge", issue_ids=source_ids,
   331→        cluster_name=target, actor="user",
   332→        detail={"source": source, "added": added},
   333→    )
   334→    save_plan(plan)
   335→    print(colorize(
   336→        f"  Merged cluster {source!r} into {target!r}: "
   337→        f"{added} issue(s) added, {len(source_ids)} total moved. Source deleted.",
   338→        "green",
   339→    ))
   340→
   341→
   342→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   343→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   344→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   345→    dispatch = {
   346→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   347→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   348→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   349→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   350→        "reorder": _cmd_cluster_reorder,
   351→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   352→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   353→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   354→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   355→    }
   356→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   357→    if handler is None:
   358→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   359→        return
   360→    handler(args)
   361→
   362→
   363→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   364→

1→"""Pattern → issue-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel, match_issues
     7→
     8→
     9→def _append_unique(fid: str, seen: set[str], result: list[str]) -> None:
    10→    if fid in seen:
    11→        return
    12→    seen.add(fid)
    13→    result.append(fid)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _collect_plan_ids(plan: PlanModel | None) -> set[str]:
    17→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    18→    if plan is None:
    19→        return plan_ids
    20→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    21→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    22→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    23→        plan_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    24→    return plan_ids
    25→
    26→
    27→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    28→    state: StateModel,
    29→    patterns: list[str],
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    32→    status_filter: str = "open",
    33→) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of issue IDs.
    35→
    36→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    37→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    38→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["issues"]``.
    39→    """
    40→    seen: set[str] = set()
    41→    result: list[str] = []
    42→    plan_ids = _collect_plan_ids(plan)
    43→
    44→    for pattern in patterns:
    45→        matches = match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    46→        if matches:
    47→            for issue in matches:
    48→                _append_unique(issue["id"], seen, result)
    49→            continue
    50→        if pattern in plan_ids:
    51→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state issues
    52→            _append_unique(pattern, seen, result)
    53→            continue
    54→        if plan is not None and pattern in plan.get("clusters", {}):
    55→            # Cluster name → expand to member IDs
    56→            for fid in plan["clusters"][pattern].get("issue_ids", []):
    57→                _append_unique(fid, seen, result)
    58→    return result
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    62→

   124→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   125→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   126→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   127→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   128→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   129→    if not has_triage:
   130→        return ""
   131→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   132→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   133→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   134→    if completed:
   135→        return (
   136→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   137→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   138→        )
   139→    return (
   140→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   141→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   142→    )
   143→

    30→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"

    32→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    33→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    34→}

    42→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    43→    created_at: str

   113→    """Frozen score snapshot captured when a plan cycle starts."""
   114→
   115→    strict: float
   116→    overall: float
   117→    objective: float
   118→    verified: float
   119→    reset: bool

   123→    """Persisted payload for one triage stage checkpoint."""
   124→
   125→    stage: str
   126→    report: str
   127→    cited_ids: list[str]
   128→    timestamp: str
   129→    issue_count: int

   136→    """Archived triage stage state captured when triage is completed."""
   137→
   138→    completed_at: str
   139→    stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   140→    strategy: str
   141→

   143→class EpicTriageMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
   144→    """Metadata persisted for the multi-stage triage flow."""
   145→
   146→    triaged_ids: list[str]
   147→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
   148→    issue_snapshot_hash: str
   149→    strategy_summary: str
   150→    trigger: str
   151→    version: int
   152→    last_run: str
   153→    last_completed_at: str
   154→    triage_stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   155→    stage_snapshot_hash: str
   156→    stage_refresh_required: bool
   157→    last_triage: LastTriageSnapshot
   158→
   159→
   160→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   161→    version: Required[int]
   162→    created: Required[str]
   163→    updated: Required[str]
   164→    queue_order: list[str]
   165→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   166→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   167→    active_cluster: str | None
   168→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   169→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   170→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   171→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   172→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   173→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   174→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   175→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   176→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   177→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   178→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   179→    # Legacy compat keys (consumed by migration helpers)
   180→    epics: NotRequired[dict[str, Any]]
   181→    epic_synthesis_meta: NotRequired[dict[str, Any]]
   182→    pending_plan_gate: NotRequired[bool]
   183→    uncommitted_findings: NotRequired[list[str]]
   184→
   185→
   186→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   187→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   188→    now = utc_now()
   189→    return {
   190→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   191→        "created": now,
   192→        "updated": now,
   193→        "queue_order": [],
   194→        "deferred": [],
   195→        "skipped": {},
   196→        "active_cluster": None,
   197→        "overrides": {},
   198→        "clusters": {},
   199→        "superseded": {},
   200→        "promoted_ids": [],
   201→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   202→        "execution_log": [],
   203→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   204→        "commit_log": [],
   205→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   206→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   212→
   213→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   214→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   215→    v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue),
   216→    and v6 (synthesis naming) to v7 (triage naming).
   217→    """
   218→    defaults = empty_plan()
   219→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   220→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   221→
   222→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   223→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   224→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   225→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   226→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   227→    _migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan)
   228→
   229→
   230→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   231→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   232→    return {
   233→        name: cluster
   234→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   235→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   236→    }
   237→
   238→
   239→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   240→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   241→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   242→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   243→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   244→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   245→
   246→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   247→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   248→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   249→    if overlap:
   250→        raise ValueError(
   251→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   255→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   256→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   257→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   258→            raise ValueError(
   259→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   260→            )
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   265→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   266→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   267→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   268→    "Cluster",
   269→    "CommitRecord",
   270→    "ItemOverride",
   271→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   272→    "PlanModel",
   273→    "PlanStartScores",
   274→    "SkipEntry",
   275→    "SupersededEntry",
   276→    "TriageStagePayload",
   277→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   278→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   279→    "empty_plan",
   280→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   281→    "triage_clusters",
   282→    "validate_plan",
   283→]
   284→

1→"""Queue mutation helpers for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import add_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→
     8→
     9→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:
    10→    """Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    11→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    12→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    13→    if issue_id in order:
    14→        order.remove(issue_id)
    15→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _resolve_position(
    19→    order: list[str],
    20→    position: str,
    21→    target: str | None = None,
    22→    offset: int | None = None,
    23→    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> int:
    25→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    26→
    27→    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    28→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    29→    """
    30→    moving = set(issue_ids or [])
    31→
    32→    if position == "top":
    33→        return 0
    34→    if position == "bottom":
    35→        return len(order)
    36→
    37→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    38→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    39→
    40→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    41→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    42→            order,
    43→            moving,
    44→            position=position,
    45→            offset=offset,
    46→            issue_ids=issue_ids,
    47→        )
    48→
    49→    return len(order)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _resolve_relative_position(
    53→    order: list[str],
    54→    moving: set[str],
    55→    *,
    56→    position: str,
    57→    target: str,
    58→) -> int:
    59→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    60→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    61→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
    62→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
    66→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
    67→        if item_id == target:
    68→            return i
    69→    return None
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_offset_position(
    73→    order: list[str],
    74→    moving: set[str],
    75→    *,
    76→    position: str,
    77→    offset: int,
    78→    issue_ids: list[str] | None,
    79→) -> int:
    80→    if not issue_ids:
    81→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
    82→    first_id = issue_ids[0]
    83→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    84→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
    85→    if current_idx is None:
    86→        if position == "up":
    87→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    88→        return len(clean_order)
    89→    if position == "up":
    90→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    91→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    92→
    93→
    94→def move_items(
    95→    plan: PlanModel,
    96→    issue_ids: list[str],
    97→    position: str,
    98→    target: str | None = None,
    99→    offset: int | None = None,
   100→) -> int:
   101→    """Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   102→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   103→
   104→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   105→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   106→
   107→    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   108→    if not issue_ids:
   109→        return 0
   110→
   111→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   112→
   113→    # Remove from skipped if present
   114→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   115→    for fid in issue_ids:
   116→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   117→
   118→    # Remove from current position in order
   119→    for fid in issue_ids:
   120→        if fid in order:
   121→            order.remove(fid)
   122→
   123→    # Resolve insertion point
   124→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)
   125→
   126→    # Insert in original order
   127→    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):
   128→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   129→
   130→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   131→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   132→    if position == "top":
   133→        add_promoted_ids(plan, issue_ids)
   134→
   135→    return len(issue_ids)
   136→
   137→
   138→__all__ = ["move_items"]
   139→

1→"""Plan reorder subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import append_log_entry, load_plan, move_items, save_plan
    12→
    13→
    14→def resolve_target(plan: dict, target: str | None, position: str) -> str | None:
    15→    """Resolve a cluster name used as a before/after target to a member ID."""
    16→    if target is None:
    17→        return None
    18→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    19→    if target not in clusters:
    20→        return target
    21→    member_ids = clusters[target].get("issue_ids", [])
    22→    if not member_ids:
    23→        return target
    24→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    25→    member_set = set(member_ids)
    26→    ordered = [fid for fid in order if fid in member_set]
    27→    if not ordered:
    28→        return member_ids[0]
    29→    return ordered[0] if position == "before" else ordered[-1]
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_reorder(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Reorder issues in the queue."""
    34→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    35→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    39→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    40→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    41→
    42→    if position in ("before", "after") and target is None:
    43→        print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires --target (-t). Example: plan reorder <pat> {position} -t <id>", "red"))
    44→        return
    45→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is None:
    46→        print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires --target (-t) with an integer offset. Example: plan reorder <pat> {position} -t 3", "red"))
    47→        return
    48→
    49→    plan = load_plan()
    50→
    51→    target = resolve_target(plan, target, position)
    52→
    53→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    54→    if not issue_ids:
    55→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    56→        return
    57→
    58→    offset: int | None = None
    59→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
    60→        try:
    61→            offset = int(target)
    62→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
    63→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
    64→            return
    65→        target = None
    66→
    67→    count = move_items(plan, issue_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
    68→    append_log_entry(
    69→        plan, "reorder", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
    70→        detail={"position": position, "target": target, "offset": offset},
    71→    )
    72→    save_plan(plan)
    73→    print(colorize(f"  Moved {count} item(s) to {position}.", "green"))
    74→
    75→
    76→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_reorder", "resolve_target"]
    77→

     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
     8→    ensure_container_types as _ensure_container_types,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
    11→    migrate_deferred_to_skipped as _migrate_deferred_to_skipped,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
    14→    migrate_epics_to_clusters as _migrate_epics_to_clusters,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
    17→    migrate_synthesis_to_triage as _migrate_synthesis_to_triage,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
    20→    migrate_v5_to_v6 as _migrate_v5_to_v6,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
    23→    normalize_cluster_defaults as _normalize_cluster_defaults,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import VALID_SKIP_KINDS
    26→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    27→
    28→PLAN_VERSION = 7
    29→
    30→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    31→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = {
    32→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    33→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    34→}
    35→
    36→
    37→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    38→    issue_id: Required[str]
    39→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    40→    reason: str | None
    41→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    42→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    43→    created_at: str
    44→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    45→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    46→
    47→
    48→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    49→    issue_id: Required[str]
    50→    description: str | None
    51→    note: str | None
    52→    cluster: str | None
    53→    created_at: str
    54→    updated_at: str
    55→
    56→
    57→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    58→    name: Required[str]
    59→    description: str | None
    60→    issue_ids: list[str]
    61→    created_at: str
    62→    updated_at: str
    63→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    64→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    65→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    66→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    67→    optional: bool
    68→    thesis: str
    69→    direction: str
    70→    root_cause: str
    71→    supersedes: list[str]
    72→    dismissed: list[str]
    73→    agent_safe: bool
    74→    dependency_order: int
    75→    action_steps: list[str]
    76→    source_clusters: list[str]
    77→    status: str
    78→    triage_version: int
    79→
    80→
    81→class CommitRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    82→    sha: Required[str]           # git commit SHA
    83→    branch: str | None           # branch name
    84→    issue_ids: list[str]       # issues included
    85→    recorded_at: str             # ISO timestamp
    86→    note: str | None             # user-provided rationale
    87→    cluster_name: str | None     # cluster context
    88→
    89→
    90→class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    91→    timestamp: Required[str]
    92→    action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
    93→    issue_ids: list[str]
    94→    cluster_name: str | None
    95→    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"
    96→    note: str | None
    97→    detail: dict[str, Any]  # action-specific extra data
    98→
    99→
   100→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
   101→    original_id: Required[str]
   102→    original_detector: str
   103→    original_file: str
   104→    original_summary: str
   105→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
   106→    superseded_at: str
   107→    remapped_to: str | None
   108→    candidates: list[str]
   109→    note: str | None
   110→
   111→
   112→class PlanStartScores(TypedDict, total=False):
   113→    """Frozen score snapshot captured when a plan cycle starts."""
   114→
   115→    strict: float
   116→    overall: float
   117→    objective: float
   118→    verified: float
   119→    reset: bool
   120→
   121→
   122→class TriageStagePayload(TypedDict, total=False):
   123→    """Persisted payload for one triage stage checkpoint."""
   124→
   125→    stage: str
   126→    report: str
   127→    cited_ids: list[str]
   128→    timestamp: str
   129→    issue_count: int
   130→    recurring_dims: list[str]
   131→    confirmed_at: str
   132→    confirmed_text: str
   133→
   134→
   135→class LastTriageSnapshot(TypedDict, total=False):
   136→    """Archived triage stage state captured when triage is completed."""
   137→
   138→    completed_at: str
   139→    stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   140→    strategy: str
   141→
   142→
   143→class EpicTriageMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
   144→    """Metadata persisted for the multi-stage triage flow."""
   145→
   146→    triaged_ids: list[str]
   147→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
   148→    issue_snapshot_hash: str
   149→    strategy_summary: str
   150→    trigger: str
   151→    version: int
   152→    last_run: str
   153→    last_completed_at: str
   154→    triage_stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   155→    stage_snapshot_hash: str
   156→    stage_refresh_required: bool
   157→    last_triage: LastTriageSnapshot
   158→
   159→
   160→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   161→    version: Required[int]
   162→    created: Required[str]
   163→    updated: Required[str]
   164→    queue_order: list[str]
   165→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   166→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   167→    active_cluster: str | None
   168→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   169→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   170→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   171→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   172→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   173→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   174→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   175→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   176→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   177→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   178→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   179→    # Legacy compat keys (consumed by migration helpers)
   180→    epics: NotRequired[dict[str, Any]]
   181→    epic_synthesis_meta: NotRequired[dict[str, Any]]
   182→    pending_plan_gate: NotRequired[bool]
   183→    uncommitted_findings: NotRequired[list[str]]
   184→
   185→
   186→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   187→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   188→    now = utc_now()
   189→    return {
   190→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   191→        "created": now,
   192→        "updated": now,
   193→        "queue_order": [],
   194→        "deferred": [],
   195→        "skipped": {},
   196→        "active_cluster": None,
   197→        "overrides": {},
   198→        "clusters": {},
   199→        "superseded": {},
   200→        "promoted_ids": [],
   201→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   202→        "execution_log": [],
   203→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   204→        "commit_log": [],
   205→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   206→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   212→
   213→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   214→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   215→    v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue),
   216→    and v6 (synthesis naming) to v7 (triage naming).
   217→    """
   218→    defaults = empty_plan()
   219→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   220→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   221→
   222→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   223→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   224→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   225→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   226→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   227→    _migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan)
   228→
   229→
   230→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   231→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   232→    return {
   233→        name: cluster
   234→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   235→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   236→    }
   237→
   238→
   239→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   240→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   241→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   242→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   243→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   244→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   245→
   246→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   247→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   248→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   249→    if overlap:
   250→        raise ValueError(
   251→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   255→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   256→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   257→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   258→            raise ValueError(
   259→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   260→            )
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   265→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   266→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   267→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   268→    "Cluster",
   269→    "CommitRecord",
   270→    "ItemOverride",
   271→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   272→    "PlanModel",
   273→    "PlanStartScores",
   274→    "SkipEntry",
   275→    "SupersededEntry",
   276→    "TriageStagePayload",
   277→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   278→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   279→    "empty_plan",
   280→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   281→    "triage_clusters",
   282→    "validate_plan",
   283→]
   284→

   124→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   125→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   126→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   127→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   128→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   129→    if not has_triage:
   130→        return ""
   131→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   132→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   133→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   134→    if completed:
   135→        return (
   136→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   137→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   138→        )
   139→    return (
   140→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   141→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   142→    )
   143→

1→"""Cluster mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_queue import move_items
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→
    10→def create_cluster(
    11→    plan: PlanModel,
    12→    name: str,
    13→    description: str | None = None,
    14→    action: str | None = None,
    15→) -> Cluster:
    16→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
    19→        raise ValueError(
    20→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
    21→        )
    22→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
    23→        raise ValueError(
    24→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}"
    25→        )
    26→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
    27→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
    28→    now = utc_now()
    29→    cluster: Cluster = {
    30→        "name": name,
    31→        "description": description,
    32→        "issue_ids": [],
    33→        "created_at": now,
    34→        "updated_at": now,
    35→        "auto": False,
    36→        "cluster_key": "",
    37→        "action": action,
    38→        "user_modified": False,
    39→    }
    40→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
    41→    return cluster
    42→
    43→
    44→def add_to_cluster(
    45→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]
    46→) -> int:
    47→    """Add issue IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
    48→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    49→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
    50→    if cluster is None:
    51→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    52→
    53→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["issue_ids"]
    54→    count = 0
    55→    now = utc_now()
    56→    for fid in issue_ids:
    57→        if fid not in member_ids:
    58→            member_ids.append(fid)
    59→            count += 1
    60→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
    61→        if fid not in overrides:
    62→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    63→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
    64→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
    65→
    66→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
    67→    return count
    68→
    69→
    70→def remove_from_cluster(
    71→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]
    72→) -> int:
    73→    """Remove issue IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
    74→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    75→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
    76→    if cluster is None:
    77→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    78→
    79→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["issue_ids"]
    80→    now = utc_now()
    81→    count = 0
    82→    for fid in issue_ids:
    83→        if fid in member_ids:
    84→            member_ids.remove(fid)
    85→            count += 1
    86→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
    87→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
    88→            override["cluster"] = None
    89→            override["updated_at"] = now
    90→
    91→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
    92→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
    93→
    94→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
    95→    return count
    96→
    97→
    98→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
    99→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   100→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   101→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   102→    if cluster is None:
   103→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   104→
   105→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    for fid in orphaned:
   108→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   109→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   110→            override["cluster"] = None
   111→            override["updated_at"] = now
   112→
   113→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   114→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   115→
   116→    return orphaned
   117→
   118→
   119→def merge_clusters(
   120→    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str
   121→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   122→    """Move all source issues to target, copy missing metadata, delete source."""
   123→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   124→    if source_name == target_name:
   125→        raise ValueError("Cannot merge a cluster into itself")
   126→    source = plan["clusters"].get(source_name)
   127→    if source is None:
   128→        raise ValueError(f"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist")
   129→    target = plan["clusters"].get(target_name)
   130→    if target is None:
   131→        raise ValueError(f"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist")
   132→
   133→    source_ids = list(source.get("issue_ids", []))
   134→    target_ids: list[str] = target["issue_ids"]
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→
   137→    existing = set(target_ids)
   138→    added = 0
   139→    for fid in source_ids:
   140→        if fid not in existing:
   141→            target_ids.append(fid)
   142→            existing.add(fid)
   143→            added += 1
   144→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   145→        if fid not in overrides:
   146→            overrides[fid] = {"issue_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   147→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = target_name
   148→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   149→
   150→    if not target.get("description") and source.get("description"):
   151→        target["description"] = source["description"]
   152→    if not target.get("action_steps") and source.get("action_steps"):
   153→        target["action_steps"] = list(source["action_steps"])
   154→    if not target.get("action") and source.get("action"):
   155→        target["action"] = source["action"]
   156→
   157→    target["updated_at"] = now
   158→
   159→    plan["clusters"].pop(source_name, None)
   160→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == source_name:
   161→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   162→
   163→    return added, source_ids
   164→
   165→
   166→def move_cluster(
   167→    plan: PlanModel,
   168→    cluster_name: str,
   169→    position: str,
   170→    target: str | None = None,
   171→    offset: int | None = None,
   172→) -> int:
   173→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   174→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   175→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   176→    if cluster is None:
   177→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   178→
   179→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   180→    if not member_ids:
   181→        return 0
   182→
   183→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   184→
   185→
   186→__all__ = [
   187→    "add_to_cluster",
   188→    "create_cluster",
   189→    "delete_cluster",
   190→    "merge_clusters",
   191→    "move_cluster",
   192→    "remove_from_cluster",
   193→]

1→"""Plan reorder subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import append_log_entry, load_plan, move_items, save_plan
    12→
    13→
    14→def resolve_target(plan: dict, target: str | None, position: str) -> str | None:
    15→    """Resolve a cluster name used as a before/after target to a member ID."""
    16→    if target is None:
    17→        return None
    18→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    19→    if target not in clusters:
    20→        return target
    21→    member_ids = clusters[target].get("issue_ids", [])
    22→    if not member_ids:
    23→        return target
    24→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    25→    member_set = set(member_ids)
    26→    ordered = [fid for fid in order if fid in member_set]
    27→    if not ordered:
    28→        return member_ids[0]
    29→    return ordered[0] if position == "before" else ordered[-1]
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_reorder(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Reorder issues in the queue."""
    34→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    35→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    39→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    40→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    41→
    42→    if position in ("before", "after") and target is None:
    43→        print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires --target (-t). Example: plan reorder <pat> {position} -t <id>", "red"))
    44→        return
    45→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is None:
    46→        print(colorize(f"  '{position}' requires --target (-t) with an integer offset. Example: plan reorder <pat> {position} -t 3", "red"))
    47→        return
    48→
    49→    plan = load_plan()
    50→
    51→    target = resolve_target(plan, target, position)
    52→
    53→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    54→    if not issue_ids:
    55→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    56→        return
    57→
    58→    offset: int | None = None
    59→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
    60→        try:
    61→            offset = int(target)
    62→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
    63→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
    64→            return
    65→        target = None
    66→
    67→    count = move_items(plan, issue_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
    68→    append_log_entry(
    69→        plan, "reorder", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
    70→        detail={"position": position, "target": target, "offset": offset},
    71→    )
    72→    save_plan(plan)
    73→    print(colorize(f"  Moved {count} item(s) to {position}.", "green"))
    74→
    75→
    76→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_reorder", "resolve_target"]
    77→

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
     9→    QueueBreakdown,
    10→    format_queue_headline,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize, print_table
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    16→    QueueBuildOptions,
    17→    build_work_queue,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    20→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_issue_ids, load_plan
    21→
    22→
    23→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    24→    if len(text) <= width:
    25→        return text
    26→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _print_queue_header(
    30→    *,
    31→    items: list[dict],
    32→    include_skipped: bool,
    33→    plan: dict,
    34→    new_count: int = 0,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    total = len(items)
    37→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    38→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    39→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    42→    subjective = sum(
    43→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    44→    )
    45→
    46→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    47→    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    48→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    49→    plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    50→
    51→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    52→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    53→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    54→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    55→        subjective=subjective,
    56→    )
    57→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    58→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    60→
    61→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster")
    62→    if focus:
    63→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    64→
    65→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    66→        return
    67→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    68→        return
    69→    print(
    70→        colorize(
    71→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    72→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    73→            "dim",
    74→        )
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    79→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    80→        return items[:top]
    81→    return items
    82→
    83→
    84→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    85→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    86→    _new = new_ids or set()
    87→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    88→        pos = str(idx)
    89→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
    90→
    91→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
    92→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    93→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    94→            conf_str = "—"
    95→            detector = "planning"
    96→            summary = f"[TRIAGE] {item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
    97→            cluster_name = ""
    98→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
    99→            conf_str = "—"
   100→            detector = "workflow"
   101→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   102→            cluster_name = ""
   103→        elif kind == "cluster":
   104→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   105→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   106→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   107→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   108→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   109→                if m.get("id") in _new
   110→            )
   111→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   112→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   113→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   114→        else:
   115→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   116→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   117→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   118→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   119→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   120→
   121→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   122→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   123→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   124→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   125→    return rows
   126→
   127→
   128→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   129→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   130→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   131→    state = runtime.state
   132→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   136→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   137→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   138→
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    print_triage_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   141→
   142→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
   143→    if not cluster_filter:
   144→        active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   145→        if active_cluster:
   146→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   147→
   148→    queue = build_work_queue(
   149→        state,
   150→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   151→            count=None,
   152→            status="open",
   153→            include_subjective=True,
   154→            plan=plan,
   155→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   156→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   157→        ),
   158→    )
   159→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   160→    # Collapse auto-clusters into display meta-items
   161→    if plan and not effective_cluster and not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   162→        items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   163→
   164→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   165→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   166→    # Merge review-based new issue IDs (since last triage)
   167→    review_new_ids = compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   168→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   169→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   170→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   171→
   172→    if sort_by == "recent":
   173→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   174→
   175→    _print_queue_header(
   176→        items=items,
   177→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   178→        plan=plan,
   179→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   180→    )
   181→
   182→    if not items:
   183→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    # Determine which items to show
   187→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   188→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   189→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   190→
   191→    print()
   192→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   193→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   194→
   195→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   196→        remaining = len(items) - top
   197→        print(colorize(
   198→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   199→        ))
   200→    print()
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   204→

1→"""Pattern → issue-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel, match_issues
     7→
     8→
     9→def _append_unique(fid: str, seen: set[str], result: list[str]) -> None:
    10→    if fid in seen:
    11→        return
    12→    seen.add(fid)
    13→    result.append(fid)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _collect_plan_ids(plan: PlanModel | None) -> set[str]:
    17→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    18→    if plan is None:
    19→        return plan_ids
    20→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    21→    plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    22→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    23→        plan_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    24→    return plan_ids
    25→
    26→
    27→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    28→    state: StateModel,
    29→    patterns: list[str],
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    32→    status_filter: str = "open",
    33→) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of issue IDs.
    35→
    36→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    37→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    38→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["issues"]``.
    39→    """
    40→    seen: set[str] = set()
    41→    result: list[str] = []
    42→    plan_ids = _collect_plan_ids(plan)
    43→
    44→    for pattern in patterns:
    45→        matches = match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    46→        if matches:
    47→            for issue in matches:
    48→                _append_unique(issue["id"], seen, result)
    49→            continue
    50→        if pattern in plan_ids:
    51→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state issues
    52→            _append_unique(pattern, seen, result)
    53→            continue
    54→        if plan is not None and pattern in plan.get("clusters", {}):
    55→            # Cluster name → expand to member IDs
    56→            for fid in plan["clusters"][pattern].get("issue_ids", []):
    57→                _append_unique(fid, seen, result)
    58→    return result
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    62→

    83→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    96→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})

    98→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))

   107→    else:

   110→    if clusters:
   111→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   112→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   113→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   115→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   116→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   117→    if described or noted:

   141→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))

   146→    )

1→"""Queue mutation helpers for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import add_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→
     8→
     9→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:
    10→    """Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    11→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    12→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    13→    if issue_id in order:
    14→        order.remove(issue_id)
    15→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _resolve_position(
    19→    order: list[str],
    20→    position: str,
    21→    target: str | None = None,
    22→    offset: int | None = None,
    23→    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> int:
    25→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    26→
    27→    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    28→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    29→    """
    30→    moving = set(issue_ids or [])
    31→
    32→    if position == "top":
    33→        return 0
    34→    if position == "bottom":
    35→        return len(order)
    36→
    37→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    38→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    39→
    40→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    41→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    42→            order,
    43→            moving,
    44→            position=position,
    45→            offset=offset,
    46→            issue_ids=issue_ids,
    47→        )
    48→
    49→    return len(order)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _resolve_relative_position(
    53→    order: list[str],
    54→    moving: set[str],
    55→    *,
    56→    position: str,
    57→    target: str,
    58→) -> int:
    59→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    60→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    61→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
    62→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
    66→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
    67→        if item_id == target:
    68→            return i
    69→    return None
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_offset_position(
    73→    order: list[str],
    74→    moving: set[str],
    75→    *,
    76→    position: str,
    77→    offset: int,
    78→    issue_ids: list[str] | None,
    79→) -> int:
    80→    if not issue_ids:
    81→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
    82→    first_id = issue_ids[0]
    83→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    84→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
    85→    if current_idx is None:
    86→        if position == "up":
    87→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    88→        return len(clean_order)
    89→    if position == "up":
    90→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    91→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    92→
    93→
    94→def move_items(
    95→    plan: PlanModel,
    96→    issue_ids: list[str],
    97→    position: str,
    98→    target: str | None = None,
    99→    offset: int | None = None,
   100→) -> int:
   101→    """Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   102→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   103→
   104→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   105→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   106→
   107→    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   108→    if not issue_ids:
   109→        return 0
   110→
   111→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   112→
   113→    # Remove from skipped if present
   114→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   115→    for fid in issue_ids:
   116→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   117→
   118→    # Remove from current position in order
   119→    for fid in issue_ids:
   120→        if fid in order:
   121→            order.remove(fid)
   122→
   123→    # Resolve insertion point
   124→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)
   125→
   126→    # Insert in original order
   127→    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):
   128→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   129→
   130→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   131→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   132→    if position == "top":
   133→        add_promoted_ids(plan, issue_ids)
   134→
   135→    return len(issue_ids)
   136→
   137→
   138→__all__ = ["move_items"]
   139→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import (
     9→    get_issue_description,
    10→    get_issue_note,
    11→    get_issue_override,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    18→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    19→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not scan_history:
    21→        return set()
    22→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    23→    if not threshold:
    24→        return set()
    25→    return {
    26→        issue_id
    27→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    28→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    29→    }
    30→
    31→
    32→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    33→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    34→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    35→
    36→    for item in items:
    37→        issue_id = item["id"]
    38→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    39→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    40→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    41→        if description:
    42→            item["plan_description"] = description
    43→        if note:
    44→            item["plan_note"] = note
    45→        if override.get("cluster"):
    46→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    47→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    48→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    49→                "name": cluster_name,
    50→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    51→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", [])),
    52→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    53→            }
    54→
    55→
    56→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    new_ids: set[str],
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    62→
    63→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    64→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    65→    """
    66→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    67→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    68→
    69→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    70→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    71→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    72→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    73→
    74→    for item in items:
    75→        item_id = item["id"]
    76→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    77→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    78→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    79→
    80→
    81→def separate_skipped(
    82→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    83→    plan: dict,
    84→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    85→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    88→    """
    89→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    90→    if not skipped_ids:
    91→        return items, []
    92→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    93→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    96→            skipped.append(item)
    97→        else:
    98→            non_skipped.append(item)
    99→    return non_skipped, skipped
   100→
   101→
   102→def filter_cluster_focus(
   103→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   104→    plan: dict,
   105→    cluster: str | None,
   106→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   109→    if not effective_cluster:
   110→        return items
   111→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   112→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   113→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   115→        return items
   116→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   117→
   118→
   119→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   120→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   121→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   122→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   123→
   124→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→
   136→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   137→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   138→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   139→    if meta:
   140→        return meta.action_type
   141→    return "manual_fix"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _build_cluster_meta(
   145→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   146→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   147→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   148→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   149→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   150→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   151→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   152→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   153→        action_type = "reorganize"
   154→    else:
   155→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   156→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   157→            action_type = "refactor"
   158→
   159→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   160→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   161→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   162→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   163→    else:
   164→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   165→
   166→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   167→    if not primary_command:
   168→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   169→
   170→    estimated_impact = max(
   171→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   172→    )
   173→
   174→    return {
   175→        "id": cluster_name,
   176→        "kind": "cluster",
   177→        "action_type": action_type,
   178→        "summary": summary,
   179→        "members": members,
   180→        "member_count": len(members),
   181→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   182→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   183→        "cluster_auto": True,
   184→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   185→        "confidence": "high",
   186→        "detector": detector,
   187→        "file": "",
   188→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   189→    }
   190→
   191→
   192→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   193→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   194→
   195→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   196→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   197→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   198→    """
   199→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   200→    if not clusters:
   201→        return items
   202→
   203→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   204→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   205→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   206→            continue
   207→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   208→            fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   209→
   210→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   211→        return items
   212→
   213→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   214→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   215→    for item in items:
   216→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   217→        if cname:
   218→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   219→
   220→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   221→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   222→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   223→        if len(members) < 2:
   224→            continue
   225→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   226→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   230→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   231→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   232→    result: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   233→    for item in items:
   234→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   235→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   236→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   237→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   238→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   239→            # skip individual member
   240→        else:
   241→            result.append(item)
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→
   245→__all__ = [
   246→    "collapse_clusters",
   247→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   248→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   249→    "new_item_ids",
   250→    "separate_skipped",
   251→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   252→    "stamp_positions",
   253→]
   254→
